type LabelProps = {
  className?: string;
  device?: "Backbone Switch" | "Firewall" | "Wireless Core (vCore)" | "MDM" | "Relay Server (PMIS)" | "Antivirus Management" | "WIPS" | "Access Control" | "SSL VPN" | "Wireless FHM (FrontHaul MUX)" | "Base Station Equipment (vCU-DU)" | "Workgroup Switch (Wireless Infra)" | "Workgroup Switch (Security)" | "Workgroup Switch (Wireless Relay)" | "Wireless Management System (2 Lines)";
};

function Label({ className, device = "Backbone Switch" }: LabelProps) {
  const isAccessControl = device === "Access Control";
  const isAntivirusManagement = device === "Antivirus Management";
  const isBaseStationEquipmentVCuDu = device === "Base Station Equipment (vCU-DU)";
  const isFirewall = device === "Firewall";
  const isMdm = device === "MDM";
  const isRelayServerPmis = device === "Relay Server (PMIS)";
  const isSslVpn = device === "SSL VPN";
  const isWips = device === "WIPS";
  const isWirelessCoreVCore = device === "Wireless Core (vCore)";
  const isWirelessFhmFrontHaulMux = device === "Wireless FHM (FrontHaul MUX)";
  const isWirelessManagementSystem2Lines = device === "Wireless Management System (2 Lines)";
  const isWorkgroupSwitchSecurity = device === "Workgroup Switch (Security)";
  const isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfra = device === "Workgroup Switch (Wireless Infra)";
  const isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfraOrWorkgroupSwitchSecurityOr = ["Workgroup Switch (Wireless Infra)", "Workgroup Switch (Security)", "Workgroup Switch (Wireless Relay)"].includes(device);
  const isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessRelay = device === "Workgroup Switch (Wireless Relay)";
  return (
    <div className={className || `bg-[#f0f4ff] border-2 border-solid content-stretch flex items-center justify-center px-[12px] py-[6px] relative rounded-[6px] ${isWirelessManagementSystem2Lines ? "border-[rgba(74,85,104,0.5)]" : isBaseStationEquipmentVCuDu ? "border-[rgba(74,85,104,0.5)] w-[132px]" : isWirelessFhmFrontHaulMux ? "border-[rgba(74,85,104,0.5)] w-[141px]" : isSslVpn ? "border-[rgba(46,106,240,0.5)] w-[85px]" : ["Antivirus Management", "Access Control"].includes(device) ? "border-[rgba(46,106,240,0.5)] w-[76px]" : isRelayServerPmis ? "border-[rgba(157,59,206,0.5)] w-[84px]" : isMdm ? "border-[rgba(46,106,240,0.5)] w-[64px]" : isWirelessCoreVCore ? "border-[rgba(74,85,104,0.5)] w-[122px]" : "border-[rgba(46,106,240,0.5)]"}`} id={isWirelessManagementSystem2Lines ? "node-17_3117" : isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessRelay ? "node-17_3112" : isWorkgroupSwitchSecurity ? "node-17_3107" : isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfra ? "node-17_3102" : isBaseStationEquipmentVCuDu ? "node-17_3097" : isWirelessFhmFrontHaulMux ? "node-17_3092" : isSslVpn ? "node-17_3082" : isAccessControl ? "node-17_3077" : isWips ? "node-17_3072" : isAntivirusManagement ? "node-17_3067" : isRelayServerPmis ? "node-17_3062" : isMdm ? "node-17_3057" : isWirelessCoreVCore ? "node-17_3052" : isFirewall ? "node-17_3042" : "node-17_3037"}>
      {["Wireless Core (vCore)", "Relay Server (PMIS)", "Wireless FHM (FrontHaul MUX)", "Base Station Equipment (vCU-DU)", "Workgroup Switch (Wireless Infra)", "Workgroup Switch (Security)", "Workgroup Switch (Wireless Relay)", "Wireless Management System (2 Lines)"].includes(device) && (
        <div className={`[word-break:break-word] leading-[0] not-italic relative shrink-0 text-[#4a5568] text-center whitespace-nowrap ${isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfraOrWorkgroupSwitchSecurityOr ? 'font-["Gothic_A1:Bold"] text-[14px] tracking-[-0.28px]' : 'font-["Pretendard:SemiBold"] text-[15px]'}`} id={isWirelessManagementSystem2Lines ? "node-17_3116" : isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessRelay ? "node-17_3111" : isWorkgroupSwitchSecurity ? "node-17_3106" : isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfra ? "node-17_3101" : isBaseStationEquipmentVCuDu ? "node-17_3096" : isWirelessFhmFrontHaulMux ? "node-17_3091" : isRelayServerPmis ? "node-17_3061" : "node-17_3051"}>
          <p className={`mb-0 ${isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfraOrWorkgroupSwitchSecurityOr ? "leading-[18px]" : "leading-[normal]"}`}>{isWirelessManagementSystem2Lines ? "무선망관리" : isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfraOrWorkgroupSwitchSecurityOr ? "A기지 무선망" : isBaseStationEquipmentVCuDu ? "무선망 기지국설비" : isWirelessFhmFrontHaulMux ? "무선망 FHM" : isRelayServerPmis ? "중계서버" : isWirelessCoreVCore ? "무선망 코어 설비" : ""}</p>
          <p className={isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfraOrWorkgroupSwitchSecurityOr ? "leading-[18px] mb-0" : "leading-[normal]"}>{isWirelessManagementSystem2Lines ? "시스템" : isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfraOrWorkgroupSwitchSecurityOr ? "워크그룹 스위치" : isBaseStationEquipmentVCuDu ? "(vCU/DU)" : isWirelessFhmFrontHaulMux ? "(FrontHaul MUX)" : isRelayServerPmis ? "(PMIS용)" : isWirelessCoreVCore ? "(vCore)" : ""}</p>
          {isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfraOrWorkgroupSwitchSecurityOr && <p className="leading-[18px]">{isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessRelay ? "(무선 중계)" : isWorkgroupSwitchSecurity ? "(정보보호)" : isWorkgroupSwitchWirelessInfra ? "(무선 인프라)" : ""}</p>}
        </div>
      )}
      {["Backbone Switch", "Firewall", "MDM", "Antivirus Management", "WIPS", "Access Control", "SSL VPN"].includes(device) && (
        <p className={`[word-break:break-word] not-italic relative shrink-0 text-[#4a5568] text-center whitespace-nowrap ${["MDM", "Antivirus Management", "WIPS", "Access Control", "SSL VPN"].includes(device) ? 'font-["Pretendard:SemiBold"] leading-[normal] text-[15px]' : 'font-["Gothic_A1:Bold"] leading-[18px] text-[14px] tracking-[-0.28px]'}`} id={isSslVpn ? "node-17_3081" : isAccessControl ? "node-17_3076" : isWips ? "node-17_3071" : isAntivirusManagement ? "node-17_3066" : isMdm ? "node-17_3056" : isFirewall ? "node-17_3041" : "node-17_3036"}>
          {isSslVpn ? "SSL VPN" : isAccessControl ? "접근제어" : isWips ? "WIPS" : isAntivirusManagement ? "백신관리" : isMdm ? "MDM" : isFirewall ? "A기지 무선망 방화벽" : "A기지 무선망 백본스위치"}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

};

function Device({ className, type = "Backbone Switch" }: DeviceProps) {

    <div className={className || `relative ${isWirelessSwitch ? "h-[73.567px] w-[120px]" : isFirewall ? "h-[111.032px] w-[110px]" : "h-[127.437px] w-[100px]"}`} id={isWirelessSwitch ? "node-1_5550" : isFirewall ? "node-1_5548" : "node-1_5544"}>
      <div className={`absolute flex items-center justify-center mix-blend-multiply ${isWirelessSwitch ? "inset-[9.61%_0_2.51%_0]" : isFirewall ?         <div className={`-scale-x-100 flex-none skew-x-30 ${isWirelessSwitch ? "h-[hypot(-66.814cqw,8.67333cqh)] rotate-56 w-[hypot(-33.186cqw,-91.3267cqh)]" : "h-[hypot(-35.6544cqw,35.6544cqh)] rotate-30 w-[hypot(-64.3456cqw,-64.3456cqh)]"}`}>
          <div className="relative size-full" data-name="Shadow">
